Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any data_structures module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 367. The projective resolution has length at most 873 with Betti numbers bounded by 3.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 367 + O(1/n^873). Summing over all modes and applying the data_structures convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Theorem 217.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Every structural decomposition deformation of the data_structures object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 884. The moduli space has dimension at most 375 with 4 connected components.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 884 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the data_structures structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 375. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
